### Ms. V.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻风</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>病个</rb><rt>bing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>白浊</rb><rt>bah-djoh</rt><rb>病个</rb><rt>bing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>摸着</rb><rt>moh-djoh</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>遭</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>污秽个</rb><rt>u-w̆e-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>无论</rb><rt>Vu-leng</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>未免</rb><rt>vi-min</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉的个</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>6</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djoh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>犯个</rb><rt>væn-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>触犯</rb><rt>cʽoh-væn</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>陷落</rb><rt>ʽæn-lôh</rt><rb>罪里</rb><rt>ze-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>亏待</rb><rt>kwʽe-dæ</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>五股之一</rb><rt>ng-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>亏待个</rb><rt>kwʽe-dæ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>戚族</rb><rt>tsʽih-dzoh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>还个</rb><rt>wæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>还个</rb><rt>wæn-go</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>诚心</rb><rt>dzing-sing</rt><rb>举上个</rb><rt>kyü-zông-go</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>驮进</rb><rt>do-tsing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>祭司个</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>诚心个</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>端正</rb><rt>tön-tsing</rt><rb>触犯</rb><rt>cʽoh-væn</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt></ruby>：

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>当时</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>抲着</rb><rt>kʽô-djoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>Dziang-fu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>gyih-tu</rt><rb>之心</rb><rt>ts-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>gyih-tu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>gyih-tu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>；

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司个</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股</rb><rt>jih-kwu</rt><rb>之一</rb><rt>ts-ih</rt><rb>大麦粉</rb><rt>do-mah-feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>浇</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>gyih-tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>诚心个</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼来</rb><rt>tsi-læ</rt><rb>瓦器皿</rb><rt>ngô-kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>水里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby>；

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拆散</rb><rt>tsʽah-sæn</rt><rb>女人个</rb><rt>nyü-nying-go</rt><rb>头发</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>gyih-tu</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>女人个</rb><rt>nyü-nying-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>咒诅个</rb><rt>tsiu-cü-go</rt><rb>苦水</rb><rt>kwʽu-shü</rt></ruby>；

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>还要</rb><rt>wa-iao</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>端正</rb><rt>tön-tsing</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>免掉</rb><rt>min-diao</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>苦水个</rb><rt>kwʽu-shü-go</rt><rb>灾难</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt></ruby>；

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>端正</rb><rt>tön-tsing</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>近身</rb><rt>gying-sing</rt></ruby>；

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>还要</rb><rt>wa-iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就有</rb><rt>ziu-yiu</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>脚腿</rb><rt>kyiah-tʽe</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>烂</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>胀</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby>；

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>诚心个</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>满足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>常例</rb><rt>djông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>许愿个</rb><rt>hyü-nyün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一篮</rb><rt>ih-læn</rt><rb>呒酵水个</rb><rt>m-kao-shü-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的</rb><rt>ʽo-tih</rt><rb>细面粉个</rb><rt>si-min-feng-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>搽的个</rb><rt>djô-tih-go</rt><rb>呒酵水</rb><rt>m-kao-shü</rt><rb>薄饼</rb><rt>boh-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>配拢个</rb><rt>pʽe-long-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>奠个</rb><rt>din-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>献上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>献上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>赎罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>箇只</rb><rt>keh-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一篮</rb><rt>ih-læn</rt><rb>呒酵水个</rb><rt>m-kao-shü-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>谢恩祭</rb><rt>zia-eng-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>配拢个</rb><rt>pʽe-long-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>奠个</rb><rt>din-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>诚心个</rb><rt>Dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>剃头</rb><rt>tʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>头发</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>安来</rb><rt>en-læ</rt><rb>谢恩祭</rb><rt>zia-eng-tsi</rt><rb>下头个</rb><rt>ʽô-deo-go</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>煮熟了</rb><rt>ts-joh-liao</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>前腿</rb><rt>zin-tʽe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>篮里</rb><rt>læn-li</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>呒酵水个</rb><rt>m-kao-shü-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安在</rb><rt>en-dzæ</rt><rb>剃头过了</rb><rt>tʽi-deo-ko-liao</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>摇一摇</rb><rt>yiao-ih-yiao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>摇个</rb><rt>yiao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>举上个</rb><rt>kyü-zông-go</rt><rb>腿</rb><rt>tʽe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>诚心个</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>尊起个</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>喝酒</rb><rt>hah-tsiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>许愿</rb><rt>hyü-nyün</rt><rb>尊起个</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>尊起个</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>满足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>常例</rb><rt>djông-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>许个</rb><rt>hyü-go</rt><rb>愿</rb><rt>nyün</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>23</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>24</sup> 『<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>赐福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>保护</rb><rt>pao-wu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>面容个</rb><rt>min-yüong-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djoh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面容</rb><rt>min-yüong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djoh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐落</rb><rt>s-lôh</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

### Ms. VII.

<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>MO-SI</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>搭好</rb><rt>tah-hao</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>物件</rb><rt>veh-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>成为</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>成为</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>当时</rb><rt>Tông-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>族长</rb><rt>dzoh-tsiang</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>数过了个</rb><rt>su-ko-liao-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>带来个</rb><rt>ta-læ-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>篷个</rb><rt>bong-go</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>六乘</rb><rt>loh-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一乘</rb><rt>ih-dzing</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>一头</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>会幕个</rb><rt>we-mo-go</rt><rb>公用</rb><rt>kong-yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>当个</rb><rt>tông-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>六乘</rb><rt>loh-dzing</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt><rb>两乘</rb><rt>liang-dzing</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>四头</rb><rt>s-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革顺</rb><rt>Kah-jing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>当个</rb><rt>tông-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt><rb>四乘</rb><rt>s-dzing</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>八头</rb><rt>pah-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米拉利</rb><rt>Mi-lah-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>当个</rb><rt>tông-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大玛</rb><rt>Yi-da-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥辖</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>圣所个</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt><rb>肩胛</rb><rt>kyin-kah</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>抬个</rb><rt>dæ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>成功个</rb><rt>dzing-kong-go</rt><rb>礼</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭坛前</rb><rt>tsi-dæn-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>成功个</rb><rt>dzing-kong-go</rt><rb>礼</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>献上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>Di-ih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚米拿达</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-mi-nô-deh-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿顺</rb><rt>Nô-jing</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>献上个</rb><rt>hyin-zông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>银盆</rb><rt>nying-beng</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>银碗</rb><rt>nying-un</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>圣所个</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>天平</rb><rt>tʽin-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的个</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>细面粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼满了个</rb><rt>tsi-mun-liao-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>85</sup> <ruby><rb>每只</rb><rt>Me-tsah</rt><rb>银盆</rb><rt>nying-beng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每只</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>银碗</rb><rt>nying-un</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>器皿个</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming-go</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>圣所个</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>天平</rb><rt>tʽin-bing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>86</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>𣥼满个</rb><rt>tsi-mun-go</rt><rb>金炉</rb><rt>kying-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十个</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>圣所个</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>天平</rb><rt>tʽin-bing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>炉个</rb><rt>lu-go</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>廿</rb><rt>nyiæn</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>87</sup> <ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>Tso</rt><rb>烧化祭个</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt><rb>十二头</rb><rt>jih-nyi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗满</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年个</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>献个</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>赎罪祭个</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>88</sup> <ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>Tso</rt><rb>谢恩祭</rb><rt>zia-eng-tsi</rt><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>廿四</rb><rt>nyiæn-s</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗满</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年个</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>成功个</rb><rt>dzing-kong-go</rt><rb>礼</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>献上个</rb><rt>hyin-zông-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>89</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>会幕里</rb><rt>we-mo-li</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>法柜</rb><rt>fah-gwe</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>台上</rb><rt>dæ-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基路冰</rb><rt>Kyi-lu-ping</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. VIII.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>点灯</rb><rt>tin-teng</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>七盏灯</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsæn-teng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>灯台</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>发光</rb><rt>fah-kwông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>点灯</rb><rt>tin-teng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>灯台</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>灯台个</rb><rt>Teng-dæ-go</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>打炼个</rb><rt>tang-lin-go</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>铸造</rb><rt>cü-zao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>座盘</rb><rt>zo-bun</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>秆子</rb><rt>ken-ts</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>打炼个</rb><rt>tang-lin-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>铸造个</rb><rt>cü-zao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看个</rb><rt>kʽen-go</rt><rb>样式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>6</sup> 「<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>分出来</rb><rt>feng-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>扎扎</rb><rt>tsah-tsah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>身体</rb><rt>sing-tʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>剃</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>全体</rb><rt>djün-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一头</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的个</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>细面粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>配拢个</rb><rt>pʽe-long-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一头</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>赎罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>召拢</rb><rt>dziao-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>按手</rb><rt>en-siu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>公献个</rb><rt>kong-hyin-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>按手</rb><rt>en-siu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>雄牛个</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo-go</rt><rb>头上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>一头</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>献拨</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>赎罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一头</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>烧化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>公个</rb><rt>kong-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>分别</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>身体</rb><rt>sing-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>献上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>会幕里</rb><rt>we-mo-li</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>替代</rb><rt>tʽi-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>头生个</rb><rt>deo-sang-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>头生个</rb><rt>deo-sang-go</rt><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>众生</rb><rt>cong-sang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>头生个</rb><rt>deo-sang-go</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>替代</rb><rt>tʽi-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>头生个</rb><rt>deo-sang-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>会幕个</rb><rt>we-mo-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>圣所</rb><rt>Sing-sô</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djoh</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>罪恶</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>献拨</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>会幕里</rb><rt>we-mo-li</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所吩咐</rb><rt>sô-feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>廿五岁</rb><rt>nyiæn-ng-shü</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>会幕里个</rb><rt>we-mo-li-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>五十岁</rb><rt>ng-jih-shü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>完毕</rb><rt>wun-pih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗再</rb><rt>feh-tsæ</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>Kʽo-yi</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不必</rb><rt>peh-pih</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ms. IX.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二年</rb><rt>di-nyi-nyin</rt><rb>正月</rb><rt>tsing-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西难</rb><rt>Si-næn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定个</rb><rt>ding-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>本月</rb><rt>peng-yüih</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>晚上</rb><rt>mæn-zông</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>定好个</rb><rt>ding-hao-go</rt><rb>礼仪</rb><rt>li-nyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西难</rb><rt>Si-næn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>正月</rb><rt>tsing-yüih</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>晚上</rb><rt>mæn-zông</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>bang-djoh</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djoh</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>当日</rb><rt>tông-nyih</rt><rb>守逾越节</rb><rt>siu-yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djoh</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>若有</rb><rt>ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尸首个</rb><rt>s-siu-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djoh</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>出门</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>远</rb><rt>yün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>二月</rb><rt>nyi-yüih</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>晚上</rb><rt>mæn-zông</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>守个</rb><rt>siu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>无酵饼</rb><rt>vu-kao-ping</rt><rb>苦菜</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司个</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>号筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代个</rb><rt>dæ-dæ-go</rt><rb>定例</rb><rt>ding-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>第二年</rb><rt>Di-nyi-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>二月</rb><rt>nyi-yüih</rt><rb>二十</rb><rt>nyi-jih</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>法幕</rb><rt>fah-mo</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>收上去</rb><rt>siu-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拔营</rb><rt>bah-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西难</rb><rt>Si-næn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>停落</rb><rt>ding-lôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>第一遭</rb><rt>di-ih-tsao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>Di-ih</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>大旗下个</rb><rt>do-gyi-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>统带个</rb><rt>tʽong-ta-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚米拿达</rb><rt>Üô-mi-nô-deh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿顺</rb><rt>Nô-jing</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>统带</rb><rt>Tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西布伦</rb><rt>Si-pu-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希伦</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-leng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>拆</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>革顺</rb><rt>Kah-jing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米拉利</rb><rt>Mi-lah-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>搬运</rb><rt>pun-yüing</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>后</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>动身个</rb><rt>dong-sing-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>大旗下个</rb><rt>do-gyi-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做其</rb><rt>tso-gyi</rt><rb>统带个</rb><rt>tʽong-ta-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>示丢珥</u>个</rb><rt>Z-tiu-r-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利蓿</rb><rt>Yi-li-soh</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>统带</rb><rt>Tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西缅</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>苏利沙太</rb><rt>Su-li-sô-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示路蔑</rb><rt>Z-lu-mih</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>统带</rb><rt>Tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>丢珥</rb><rt>Tiu-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利雅萨</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哥辖</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>抬勒</rb><rt>dæ-leh</rt><rb>圣所</rb><rt>sing-sô</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>未到</rb><rt>vi-tao</rt><rb>之前</rb><rt>ts-zin</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>搭好</rb><rt>tah-hao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>动身个</rb><rt>dong-sing-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-feh-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>大旗下个</rb><rt>do-gyi-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>统带个</rb><rt>tʽong-ta-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚米忽</rb><rt>Üô-mi-hwah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙玛</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-mô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>统带</rb><rt>Tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比大蓿</rb><rt>Pi-da-soh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦玛列</rb><rt>Kyüô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>统带</rb><rt>Tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅悯</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>其田尼</rb><rt>Gyi-din-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚比但</rb><rt>Üô-pi-dæn</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>顶</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>结煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>动身个</rb><rt>dong-sing-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>但</rb><rt>Dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>大旗下个</rb><rt>do-gyi-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>统带个</rb><rt>tʽong-ta-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚米沙太</rb><rt>Üô-mi-sô-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚歇斯</rb><rt>Üô-hyih-s</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>统带</rb><rt>Tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚设</rb><rt>Üô-shih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>恶兰</rb><rt>Ôh-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帕结</rb><rt>Pʽô-kyih</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>统带</rb><rt>Tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>纳大利</rb><rt>Nah-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以南</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-nen-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚希拉</rb><rt>Üô-hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>各队兵</rb><rt>kôh-de-ping</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流珥</rb><rt>Liu-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿巴</rb><rt>O-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗去</rb><rt>feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>如何</rb><rt>jü-ʽo</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>眼目</rb><rt>ngæn-moh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>do-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>法柜</rb><rt>fah-gwe</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>安息个</rb><rt>en-sih-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>拔营</rb><rt>bah-ying</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>日里</rb><rt>nyih-li</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>法柜</rb><rt>Fah-gwe</rt><rb>上去个</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>法柜</rb><rt>Fah-gwe</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>千千</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsʽin</rt><rb>万万</rb><rt>væn-væn</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Ms. XI.

<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>PAH-SING</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>发怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>着来</rb><rt>dziah-læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>烧掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>诉苦</rb><rt>su-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>熓了</rb><rt>u-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>着</rb><rt>dziah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大比剌</rb><rt>Da-pi-lah</rt></ruby></u>。（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>烧</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>。）

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>外族个</rb><rt>nga-dzoh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>贪吃个</rb><rt>tʽen-kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>号泪</rb><rt>ʽao-li</rt><rb>大哭</rb><rt>da-kʽoh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>黄瓜</rb><rt>wông-kwô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>西瓜</rb><rt>si-kwô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>韭菜</rb><rt>kyiu-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>葱</rb><rt>tsʽong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>大蒜</rb><rt>da-sön</rt></ruby>；

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>精力</rb><rt>tsing-lih</rt><rb>衰败</rb><rt>sæ-ba</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-liao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>吗嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>吗嗱</rb><rt>Mô-nô</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>豆蔻</rb><rt>deo-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>颜色</rb><rt>ngæn-seh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>珠子</rb><rt>cü-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>撮勒来</rb><rt>tsʽah-leh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>磨</rb><rt>mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>舂</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>捣臼</rb><rt>tao-gyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>煮</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>镬里</rb><rt>ʽoh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的个</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>夜里</rb><rt>Yia-li</rt><rb>露水</rb><rt>lu-shü</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吗嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>随勒</rb><rt>ze-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>极其</rb><rt>gyih-gyi</rt><rb>发怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>弗好</rb><rt>feh-hao</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>赐恩</rb><rt>s-eng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>担当</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>责任</rb><rt>tsah-zing</rt></ruby>？

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>怀</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>致使得</rb><rt>cü-s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>抱</rb><rt>bao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>怀里</rb><rt>gwa-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>父母</rb><rt>vu-meo</rt><rb>抱</rb><rt>bao</rt><rb>吃奶个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-na-go</rt><rb>婴孩</rb><rt>ing-ʽæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>列祖个</rb><rt>lih-tsu-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>责任</rb><rt>tsah-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>担当</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>弗起</rb><rt>feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗如</rb><rt>feh-jü</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倘然</rb><rt>tʽông-jün</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>免掉</rb><rt>min-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>召拢</rb><rt>dziao-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>官府个</rb><rt>kwun-fu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>立来</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>降临</rb><rt>kông-ling</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>放来</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>担当</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>责任</rb><rt>tsah-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>尔自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>独立</rb><rt>doh-lih</rt><rb>担当</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>成为</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-tô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>哭泣</rb><rt>kʽoh-kyʽih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好好个</rb><rt>hao-hao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> 『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗单只</rb><rt>feh-tæn-tsih</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>两日</rb><rt>liang-nyih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>五日</rb><rt>ng-nyih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>十日</rb><rt>jih-nyih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>廿日</rb><rt>nyiæn-nyih</rt></ruby>；

<sup>20</sup> 『<ruby><rb>直</rb><rt>Dzih</rt><rb>吃到</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tao</rt><rb>一个月</rb><rt>ih-ko-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>甚至于</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>鼻头管</rb><rt>bih-deo-kwun</rt><rb>喷出来</rb><rt>pʽeng-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>厌憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>住来</rb><rt>djü-læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央个</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>哭泣</rb><rt>kʽoh-kyʽih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？』」

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六十万</rb><rt>loh-jih-væn</rt><rb>步兵</rb><rt>bu-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一个月</rb><rt>ih-ko-yüih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>全队</rb><rt>djün-de</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全队</rb><rt>djün-de</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃饱</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>收拢</rb><rt>siu-long</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃饱</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>力</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>足</rb><rt>tsoh</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>应验</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>应验</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>召拢</rb><rt>dziao-long</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>云里</rb><rt>yüing-li</rt><rb>降临</rb><rt>kông-ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>七十个</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>灵个</rb><rt>ling-go</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>Gyi-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一名</rb><rt>ih-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利达</rb><rt>Li-deh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一名</rb><rt>ih-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米达</rb><rt>Mi-deh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>营寨里</rb><rt>ying-dza-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>写落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>少年</rb><rt>siao-nyin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>奔来</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>利达</rb><rt>Li-deh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>米达</rb><rt>Mi-deh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>妒忌</rb><rt>tu-gyi</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>愿</rb><rt>Nyün</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>营寨里</rb><rt>ying-dza-li</rt></ruby>。
