<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>发起</rb><rt>fah-kyʽi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>鹌鹑鸟</rb><rt>in-jing-tiao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>约来</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt><rb>之内</rb><rt>ts-nen</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>叠来</rb><rt>diah-læ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二尺</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽah</rt><rb>厚</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>整日</rb><rt>tsing-nyih</rt><rb>整夜</rb><rt>tsing-yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>第二日</rb><rt>di-nyi-nyih</rt><rb>整日</rb><rt>tsing-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt><rb>鹌鹑鸟</rb><rt>in-jing-tiao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>撮勒</rb><rt>tsʽah-leh</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十</rb><rt>jih</rt><rb>贺梅<u>珥</rb><rt>ʽo-me-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>铺开</rb><rt>pʽu-kʽæ</rt><rb>在营寨</rb><rt>dzæ-ying-dza</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>肉</rb><rt>Nyüoh</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>牙齿里</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>嚼糊</rb><rt>ziah-wu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>大怒</rb><rt>da-nu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>私欲</rb><rt>s-yüoh</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>乞律夏大法</rb><rt>Kyʽih-lih-yüô-da-fah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈洗录</rb><rt>Ha-si-loh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈洗录</rb><rt>Ha-si-loh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-loh</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ms. XII.

<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>MO-SI</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古实</rb><rt>Kwu-jih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古实</rb><rt>Kwu-jih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>讲过</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>温柔</rb><rt>w̆eng-ziu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>降临</rb><rt>kông-ling</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>云柱</rb><rt>yüing-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>Tsiang-bong</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>若有</rb><rt>ziah-yiu</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>异像</rb><rt>yi-ziang</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>梦里</rb><rt>mong-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>对口</rb><rt>te-kʽeo</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>隐藏</rb><rt>ing-dzông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>亲眼</rb><rt>tsʽing-ngæn</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>发怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>云</rb><rt>Yüing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>Tsiang-bong</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻风</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雪</rb><rt>shih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻风</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt></ruby>；

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>愚笨个</rb><rt>nyü-beng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>Gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>娘胎</rb><rt>nyiang-tʽæ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>半身</rb><rt>pun-sing</rt><rb>烂糊</rb><rt>læn-wu</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>哀求</rb><rt>æ-gyiu</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>求恳</rb><rt>Gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>父亲</rb><rt>vu-tsʽing</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>馋唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>唾</rb><rt>tʽu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt><rb>关出</rb><rt>kwæn-cʽih</rt><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>营寨里</rb><rt>ying-dza-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>关出</rb><rt>kwæn-cʽih</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>带进</rb><rt>ta-tsing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈洗录</rb><rt>Ha-si-loh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ms. XIII.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窥探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>牧伯个</rb><rt>moh-pah-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>属</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>撒格</u>个</rb><rt>Sah-kah-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>沙母亚</rb><rt>Sô-meo-üô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>属</rb><rt>Joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西缅</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>何利</u>个</rb><rt>ʽO-li-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>沙法</rb><rt>Sô-fah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>属</rb><rt>Joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孚尼</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>属</rb><rt>Joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以萨迦</rb><rt>Yi-sah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约色</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以甲</rb><rt>Yi-kyiah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>属</rb><rt>Joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-feh-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿书亚</rb><rt>Ah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>属</rb><rt>Joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅悯</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉孚</rb><rt>Lah-fu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>八氐</rb><rt>Pah-ti</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>属</rb><rt>Joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西布伦</rb><rt>Si-pu-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>锁底</rb><rt>So-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦铁</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>属</rb><rt>Joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>苏西</rb><rt>Su-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦底</rb><rt>Kyüô-ti</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>属</rb><rt>Joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>但</rb><rt>Dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>其马利</rb><rt>Gyi-mô-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚米利</rb><rt>Üô-mi-li</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>属</rb><rt>Joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚设</rb><rt>Üô-shih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米迦勒</rb><rt>Mi-kyüô-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西帖</rb><rt>Si-tʽiah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>属</rb><rt>Joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>纳大利</rb><rt>Nah-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>博西</rb><rt>Poh-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿比</rb><rt>Nô-pi</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>属</rb><rt>Joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛其</rb><rt>Mô-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>臼利</rb><rt>Gyiu-li</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差去</rb><rt>tsʽa-kyʽi</rt><rb>窥探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿书亚</rb><rt>Ah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窥探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>望</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>山路</rb><rt>sæn-lu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>庉的个</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>刚强</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉的个</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>住个</rb><rt>djü-go</rt><rb>地段</rb><rt>di-dön</rt><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>坚固个</rb><rt>kyin-kwu-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>厚实</rb><rt>ʽeo-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>敲掐</rb><rt>kʽao-kʽah</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>树木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放大</rb><rt>fông-do</rt><rb>胆子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>地土个</rb><rt>di-tʽu-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>驮点</rb><rt>do-tin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>初</rb><rt>tsʽu</rt><rb>熟</rb><rt>joh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 贫瘠。本字不明</sup>

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窥探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>汛</rb><rt>Sing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利哈</rb><rt>Li-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈马</rb><rt>Ha-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>望</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>介去</rb><rt>ka-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚衲</rb><rt>Üô-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚希幔</rb><rt>Üô-hyi-mæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>示筛</rb><rt>Z-s</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>达买</rb><rt>Dah-mæ</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国个</rb><rt>koh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>琐安</rb><rt>So-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>建造个</rb><rt>kyin-zao-go</rt><rb>七年</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以实各</rb><rt>Yi-jih-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>斩落</rb><rt>tsæn-lôh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一球</rb><rt>ih-gyiu</rt><rb>紫葡萄</rb><rt>ts-bu-dao</rt><rb>结的个</rb><rt>kyih-tih-go</rt><rb>桠枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>杠子</rb><rt>kông-ts</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>石榴</rb><rt>zih-liu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>无花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>斩落</rb><rt>tsæn-lôh</rt><rb>一球</rb><rt>ih-gyiu</rt><rb>紫葡萄</rb><rt>ts-bu-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以实各</rb><rt>Yi-jih-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt></ruby>。（<u><ruby><rb>以实各</rb><rt>Yi-jih-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>翻出来</rb><rt>fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>一球</rb><rt>ih-gyiu</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。）

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窥探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走转</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会个</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旷野个</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦铁</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>回报</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-liao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真正</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>奶</rb><rt>na</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蜜糖个</rb><rt>mih-dông-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>结个</rb><rt>kyih-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉的个</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>刚强</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚衲</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-neh-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚玛力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>住来</rb><rt>djü-læ</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Hah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉来</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>山上</rb><rt>sæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>住来</rb><rt>djü-læ</rt><rb>海边</rb><rt>hæ-pin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河边</rb><rt>ʽo-pin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>着实</rb><rt>dziah-jih</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>得胜</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去过</rb><rt>kyʽi-ko</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>刚强</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>回报</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窥探个</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窥探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吞吃</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>身体</rb><rt>sing-tʽi</rt><rb>长大个</rb><rt>dziang-do-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚衲</rb><rt>Üô-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>衲非莲</rb><rt>Neh-fi-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>蝗虫</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Ms. XIV.

<ruby><rb>当时</rb><rt>TÔNG-Z</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>吵闹</rb><rt>tsʽao-nao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇夜</rb><rt>keh-yia</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>号哭</rb><rt>ʽao-kʽoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>弗如</rb><rt>feh-jü</rt><rb>死来</rb><rt>si-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗如</rb><rt>feh-jü</rt><rb>死来</rb><rt>si-læ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>倒弗如</rb><rt>tao-feh-jü</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>彼此</rb><rt>pe-tsʽ</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会个</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孚尼</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>窥探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>在内</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扯开</rb><rt>tsʽô-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>窥探个</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>极其</rb><rt>gyih-gyi</rt><rb>肥美</rb><rt>vi-me</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真正</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>奶</rb><rt>na</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蜜糖个</rb><rt>mih-dông-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>切弗可</rb><rt>tsʽih-feh-kʽo</rt><rb>悖逆</rb><rt>be-nyih</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>吞吃</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>保护</rb><rt>pao-wu</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>轻看</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>刚强</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>此地个</rb><rt>tsʽ-di-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>对面</rb><rt>te-min</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>遮盖</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>日里</rb><rt>nyih-li</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>云柱</rb><rt>yüing-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夜里</rb><rt>yia-li</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>火柱</rb><rt>ho-djü</rt><rb>引导</rb><rt>ying-dao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>名声</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>别国个</rb><rt>bih-koh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>16</sup> 『<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>缓缓</rb><rt>wun-wun</rt><rb>发怒个</rb><rt>fah-nu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大慈</rb><rt>da-dz</rt><rb>大悲</rb><rt>da-pe</rt><rb>赦免</rb><rt>sô-min</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>过犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>断</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>释放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>恶人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>罚</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>父个</rb><rt>vu-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>三四</rb><rt>sæn-s</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>求恳</rb><rt>Gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>da</rt><rb>慈悲</rb><rt>dz-pe</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>赦免</rb><rt>sô-min</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>赦免</rb><rt>sô-min</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>永活个</rb><rt>üong-weh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>充满</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>虽然</rb><rt>se-jün</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>试探</rb><rt>s-tʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>十遭</rb><rt>jih-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依顺</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>列祖</rb><rt>lih-tsu</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>轻看</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽen</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>各样</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>跟从</rb><rt>keng-dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>去过个</rb><rt>kyʽi-ko-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚玛力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>住来</rb><rt>djü-læ</rt><rb>山岙</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>倒走转</rb><rt>tao-tseo-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>红</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>27</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>凶恶</rb><rt>hyüong-oh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>听见了</rb><rt>tʽing-kyin-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>永活个</rb><rt>üong-weh-go</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耳朵里</rb><rt>ng-tô-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>数个</rb><rt>su-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>总数</rb><rt>tsong-su</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>廿岁</rb><rt>nyiæn-shü</rt><rb>以上</rb><rt>yi-zông</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>Pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>庉个</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶孚尼</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>进去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>飘流</rb><rt>pʽiao-liu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>担当</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>悖逆个</rb><rt>be-nyih-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>消灭</rb><rt>siao-mih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>窥探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>箇地个</rb><rt>keh-di-go</rt><rb>日数</rb><rt>nyih-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抵</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>担当</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>话过</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>凶恶</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差去</rb><rt>tsʽa-kyʽi</rt><rb>窥探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>回报</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>回报</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>弗好</rb><rt>feh-hao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djoh</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>窥探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孚尼</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>极其</rb><rt>gyih-gyi</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>早晨</rb><rt>tsao-jing</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-ting</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>情愿</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>顺流</rb><rt>jing-liu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>Feh-iao</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>免得</rb><rt>min-teh</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>打败</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt></ruby>。

<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚玛力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>竟是介</rb><rt>kying-z-ka</rt><rb>放开</rb><rt>fông-kʽæ</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>胆子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山顶头</rb><rt>sæn-ting-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>法柜</rb><rt>fah-gwe</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>。

<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt><rb>庉在</rb><rt>deng-dzæ</rt><rb>山上</rb><rt>sæn-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚玛力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>落来</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打败</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>追</rb><rt>tse</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>盍马</rb><rt>ʽEh-mô</rt></ruby></u>。

### Ms. XV.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>居住</rb><rt>kyü-djü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>献祭</rb><rt>hyin-tsi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>烧化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还愿</rb><rt>wæn-nyün</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心愿</rb><rt>sing-nyün</rt><rb>祭个</rb><rt>tsi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>定好个</rb><rt>ding-hao-go</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>牛队</rb><rt>ngeo-de</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>羊队</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>喷香</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4, 5</sup> <ruby><rb>献</rb><rt>Hyin</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>烧化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭个</rb><rt>tsi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>细面粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>四股之一</rb><rt>s-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>奠</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>四股之一</rb><rt>s-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>面粉</rb><rt>min-feng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股之二</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>；

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>奠</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>喷香个</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>烧化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>无论</rb><rt>vu-leng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>还愿</rb><rt>wæn-nyün</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谢恩</rb><rt>zia-eng</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>细面粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股之三</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>半欣</rb><rt>pun-hying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>奠</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>半欣</rb><rt>pun-hying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>喷香个</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>烧化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>每只</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每只</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>献个</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>喷香个</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>样子</rb><rt>yiang-ts</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>外来</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央个</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>喷香</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>外来</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一例个</rb><rt>ih-li-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt><rb>永远个</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>外来</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央个</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt><rb>外来</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>礼仪</rb><rt>li-nyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>吃</rb><rt>Kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>麦食</rb><rt>mah-zih</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>尝新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>麦粉</rb><rt>mah-feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>晒场里个</rb><rt>sa-dziang-li-go</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>尝新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>麦粉</rb><rt>mah-feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。
