### Ms. XVI.

<ruby><rb><u>利未</u>个</rb><rt>LI-VI-GO</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>哥辖</u>个</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh-go</rt><rb>孙子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以士哈</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-z-hah-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利押</rb><rt>Yi-li-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大丹</rb><rt>Da-tæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚比兰</rb><rt>Üô-pi-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>比拉</u>个</rb><rt>Pi-lah-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安</rb><rt>En</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>结党</rb><rt>kyih-tông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>二百五十</rb><rt>nyi-pah-ng-jih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>召拢</rb><rt>dziao-long</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>名声个</rb><rt>ming-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>过份</rb><rt>ko-veng</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>个个</rb><rt>ko-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>成为</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>圣善个</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>抬举</rb><rt>dæ-kyü</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>之</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一党</rb><rt>ih-tông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>诚心个</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>拣选个</rb><rt>kæn-shün-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一党</rb><rt>ih-tông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>香炉</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>Ming-tsiao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>香炉</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>烧</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>诚心</rb><rt>dzing-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔<u>拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>利</u>未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>过份</rb><rt>ko-veng</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔<u>拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>利</u>未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>挑选</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>小事</rb><rt>siao-z</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>近拢来</rb><rt>gying-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>祭司个</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一党</rb><rt>ih-tông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利押</rb><rt>Yi-li-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大丹</rb><rt>Da-tæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚比兰</rb><rt>Üô-pi-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>流奶</rb><rt>liu-na</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蜜糖个</rb><rt>mih-dông-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>小事</rb><rt>siao-z</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>管治</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>流奶</rb><rt>liu-na</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蜜糖个</rb><rt>mih-dông-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>葡萄园</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>𫦉</rb><rt>l̆eo</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>断断</rb><rt>tön-tön</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>上来</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>发怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>Feh-iao</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>lu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>Ming-tsiao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一党</rb><rt>ih-tông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>香炉</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>香炉</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>香炉</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>香炉</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>香炉</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>21</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>分别</rb><rt>feng-bih</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我要</rb><rt>Ngô-iao</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>性命个</rb><rt>sing-ming-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>灵个</rb><rt>ling-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>发怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>远开</rb><rt>yün-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>大丹</rb><rt>Da-tæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚比兰</rb><rt>Üô-pi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立起来</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大丹</rb><rt>Da-tæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚比兰</rb><rt>Üô-pi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>Gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>恶人个</rb><rt>ôh-nying-go</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>摸着</rb><rt>moh-djoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>大丹</rb><rt>Da-tæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚比兰</rb><rt>Üô-pi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帐篷个</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-go</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>大丹</rb><rt>Da-tæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚比兰</rb><rt>Üô-pi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>儿女</rb><rt>r-nyü</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>做样</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>死法</rb><rt>si-fah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>开口</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽeo</rt><rb>吞吃</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>活个</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>堕落</rb><rt>do-lôh</rt><rb>阴间</rb><rt>ing-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>可</rb><rt>kʽo</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>藐视</rb><rt>miao-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>将要</rb><rt>tsiang-iao</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>立的个</rb><rt>lih-tih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>豁开</rb><rt>hweh-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>开口</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽeo</rt><rb>吞吃</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>家小</rb><rt>kô-siao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>哥拉</u>个</rb><rt>Ko-lah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>活个</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>堕落</rb><rt>do-lôh</rt><rb>阴间</rb><rt>ing-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>合拢</rb><rt>ʽeh-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>Dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大声</rb><rt>da-sing</rt><rb>号哭</rb><rt>ʽao-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>逃散</rb><rt>dao-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>吞吃</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤈦煞</rb><rt>me-sah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>香个</rb><rt>hyiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>37</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>撮起</rb><rt>tsʽah-kyʽi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>香炉</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>散</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>香炉</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>诚心个</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>丧命个</rb><rt>sông-ming-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>香炉</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>打成</rb><rt>tang-dzing</rt><rb>叶子</rb><rt>yih-ts</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭坛个</rb><rt>tsi-dæn-go</rt><rb>盖</rb><rt>kæ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>诚心个</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>记号</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撮起</rb><rt>tsʽah-kyʽi</rt><rb>𤈦煞个</rb><rt>me-sah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>献个</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>铜</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>香炉</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打成</rb><rt>tang-dzing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭坛个</rb><rt>tsi-dæn-go</rt><rb>盖</rb><rt>kæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>Tso</rt><rb>记号</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>外族</rb><rt>nga-dzoh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>烧</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>免得</rb><rt>min-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一党</rb><rt>ih-tông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>灭亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>公会</rb><rt>Kong-we</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>争论个</rb><rt>tsang-leng-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>现出来</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>45</sup> 「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>驮</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>香炉</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>坛上个</rb><rt>dæn-zông-go</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>加上</rb><rt>kô-zông</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>驮到</rb><rt>do-tao</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>赎</rb><rt>joh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大怒</rb><rt>da-nu</rt><rb>发出来</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>发作了</rb><rt>fah-tsoh-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>47</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奔进</rb><rt>peng-tsing</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>发作了</rb><rt>fah-tsoh-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>烧</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>赎</rb><rt>joh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>；

<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>活人</rb><rt>weh-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>当中</rb><rt>tông-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>止住了</rb><rt>ts-djü-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>遭着</rb><rt>Tsao-djôh</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一万</rb><rt>ih-væn</rt><rb>四千</rb><rt>s-tsʽin</rt><rb>七百</rb><rt>tsʽih-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>。

<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>止住了</rb><rt>ts-djü-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ms. XVII.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每族</rb><rt>me-dzoh</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>根</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>各人个</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>利未</u>个</rb><rt>Li-vi-go</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>各族个</rb><rt>kôh-dzoh-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>法柜</rb><rt>fah-gwe</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相会个</rb><rt>siang-we-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>挑选个</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>抽芽</rb><rt>tsʽiu-ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>止住了</rb><rt>ts-djü-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>根</rb><rt>keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>之中</rb><rt>ts-cong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>法幕里</rb><rt>fah-mo-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>于是乎</rb><rt>Ü-z-wu</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>法幕里</rb><rt>fah-mo-li</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>抽芽</rb><rt>tsʽiu-ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>开花</rb><rt>kʽæ-hwô</rt><rb>结出</rb><rt>kyih-cʽih</rt><rb>熟个</rb><rt>joh-go</rt><rb>杏仁</rb><rt>ʽang-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>驮出来</rb><rt>do-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>法柜</rb><rt>fah-gwe</rt><rb>前</rb><rt>zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>悖逆个</rb><rt>be-nyih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>记号</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>止住</rb><rt>ts-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>免得</rb><rt>min-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>灭亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>呒法</rb><rt>m-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>呒法</rb><rt>m-fah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>灭亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

---

### Ms. XVIII.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>担当</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>凡</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>圣所个</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>担当</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>凡</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>职份个</rb><rt>tsih-veng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>父亲个</rb><rt>vu-tsʽing-go</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>连拢</rb><rt>lin-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>法幕</rb><rt>fah-mo</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>圣所个</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>免得</rb><rt>min-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>联合</rb><rt>lin-ʽeh</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>侍值</rb><rt>z-dzih</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>外来</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>圣所</rb><rt>sing-sô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>大怒</rb><rt>da-nu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>之中</rb><rt>ts-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>挑选</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>会幕个</rb><rt>we-mo-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>关着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>幔帐</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>侍值</rb><rt>z-dzih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>祭司个</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>服侍个</rb><rt>voh-z-go</rt><rb>赏赐</rb><rt>sông-s</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>外来</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>近拢来</rb><rt>gying-long-læ</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>举上</rb><rt>Kyü-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>成为</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>圣善个</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>傅过</rb><rt>fu-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>定为</rb><rt>ding-we</rt><rb>永远个</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>门份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>献个</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>赎愆</rb><rt>joh-kyʽin</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-lôh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>烧个</rb><rt>siao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>成为</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>圣善个</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>圣善个</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>男子</rb><rt>nen-ts</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>成为</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>圣善个</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>举上个</rb><rt>kyü-zông-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿女</rb><rt>r-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>永远个</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>门份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>洁净个</rb><rt>kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>顶好</rb><rt>Ting-hao</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顶好</rb><rt>ting-hao</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>麦</rb><rt>mah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>献拨</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>尝新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>初熟个</rb><rt>tsʽu-joh-go</rt><rb>土产</rb><rt>tʽu-tsʽæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>带来</rb><rt>ta-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>洁净个</rb><rt>kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>尊起个</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>血气个</rb><rt>hyüih-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>头生个</rb><rt>deo-sang-go</rt><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>献拨</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>头生</rb><rt>deo-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>牲口个</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo-go</rt><rb>头生</rb><rt>deo-sang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>取赎个</rb><rt>cʽü-joh-go</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>一月</rb><rt>ih-yüih</rt><rb>以外</rb><rt>yi-nga</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>估个</rb><rt>kwu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>圣所个</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>天平</rb><rt>tʽin-bing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>二十个</rb><rt>nyi-jih-go</rt><rb>季拉</rb><rt>kyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>头生个</rb><rt>deo-sang-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>头生个</rb><rt>deo-sang-go</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>诚心个</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>坛上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤈦</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>喷香个</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>火祭</rb><rt>ho-tsi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>摇个</rb><rt>yiao-go</rt><rb>前夹心</rb><rt>zin-keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>顺只腿</rb><rt>jing-tsah-tʽe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>献拨</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>诚心个</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>儿女</rb><rt>r-nyü</rt><rb>定为</rb><rt>ding-we</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>门份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>立个</rb><rt>lih-go</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>盐约</rb><rt>yin-iah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>份子</rb><rt>veng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>份子</rb><rt>veng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>当个</rb><rt>tông-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>会幕个</rb><rt>we-mo-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>免得</rb><rt>min-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>凡</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>会幕个</rb><rt>we-mo-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>担当</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代个</rb><rt>dæ-dæ-go</rt><rb>定例</rb><rt>ding-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>献拨</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>Dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>26</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业个</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>献拨</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>献上个</rb><rt>hyin-zông-go</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>晒场个</rb><rt>sa-dziang-go</rt><rb>谷</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>酒榨</rb><rt>tsiu-tsô</rt><rb>满出来个</rb><rt>mun-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>诚心个</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>一股</rb><rt>ih-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>顶好</rb><rt>ting-hao</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>献上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>晒场</rb><rt>sa-dziang</rt><rb>多起来个</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ-go</rt><rb>谷</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>酒榨</rb><rt>tsiu-tsô</rt><rb>满出来个</rb><rt>mun-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>家眷</rb><rt>kô-kyün</rt><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>差使个</rb><rt>tsʽa-s-go</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>驮出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>顶好个</rb><rt>ting-hao-go</rt><rb>献拨</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>呒罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>成为</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>圣善个</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。』」

---

### Ms. XIX.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>律法个</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>规条</rb><rt>kwe-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>一头</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>呒毛病</rb><rt>m-mao-bing</rt><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>揹轭个</rb><rt>pe-ah-ko</rt><rb>纯</rb><rt>jing</rt><rb>红色</rb><rt>ʽong-seh</rt><rb>雌牛</rb><rt>tsʽ-ngeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>牵到</rb><rt>kyʽin-tao</rt><rb>营寨外</rb><rt>ying-dza-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>指头</rb><rt>ts-deo</rt><rb>揾</rb><rt>w̆eng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>七遭</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>雌牛</rb><rt>tsʽ-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>皮</rb><rt>bi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>柏香</rb><rt>pah-hyiang</rt><rb>木</rb><rt>moh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牛膝草</rb><rt>ngeo-sih-tsʽao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>红绒</rb><rt>ʽong-nyüong</rt><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>𤈦牛个</rb><rt>me-ngeo-go</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又要</rb><rt>yi-iao</rt><rb>滰肉</rb><rt>gyiang-nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗洁净</rb><rt>feh-kyih-zing</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>。
