### Ms. XX.

<ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH-GO</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>正月里</rb><rt>tsing-yüih-li</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>汛</u>个</rb><rt>Sing-go</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦铁</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>死在</rb><rt>si-dzæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt><rb>葬好</rb><rt>tsông-hao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>公会</rb><rt>Kong-we</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>死在</rb><rt>si-dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>！

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上来</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>弗好</rb><rt>feh-hao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>弗好</rb><rt>feh-hao</rt><rb>种田</rb><rt>cong-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>无花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫葡萄</rb><rt>ts-bu-dao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>石榴</rb><rt>zih-liu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>8</sup> 「<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>Do-leh</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聚集</rb><rt>jü-jih</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>悖逆个</rb><rt>Be-nyih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>两记</rb><rt>liang-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>顶多</rb><rt>ting-to</rt><rb>流出来</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>尊</rb><rt>tseng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>圣善来</rb><rt>sing-jün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利巴</rb><rt>Mi-li-pô</rt></ruby></u>，（<ruby><rb>翻出来</rb><rt>Fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>争</rb><rt>Tsang</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。）

<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦铁</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以东</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰着个</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-go</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>庉来</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>长久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>顶凶</rb><rt>ting-hyüong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>哀求</rb><rt>æ-gyiu</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打发</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>境界</rb><rt>kying-ka</rt><rb>边沿个</rb><rt>pin-yin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦铁</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>Gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>葡萄园里</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>井里个</rb><rt>tsing-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>大路</rb><rt>do-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>岔到</rb><rt>tsʽô-tao</rt><rb>左边</rb><rt>tso-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>岔到</rb><rt>tsʽô-tao</rt><rb>右边</rb><rt>yiu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以东</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>大路</rb><rt>do-lu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>价钿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只要</rb><rt>tsih-iao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>步行</rb><rt>bu-ʽang</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>断</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>以东</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>率领</rb><rt>sah-ling</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>大力量</rb><rt>do-lih-liang</rt><rb>阻挡</rb><rt>tsu-tông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以东</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>弗许</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦铁</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何珥</rb><rt>ʽO-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以东</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何珥</rb><rt>ʽO-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山上</rb><rt>sæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>24</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米利巴</u>个</rb><rt>Mi-li-pô-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何珥</rb><rt>ʽO-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>脱掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>衣服</rb><rt>i-voh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>穿在</rb><rt>cʽün-dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>死在</rb><rt>si-dzæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何珥</rb><rt>ʽO-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>脱掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>衣服</rb><rt>i-voh</rt><rb>穿在</rb><rt>cʽün-dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>死在</rb><rt>si-dzæ</rt><rb>山上</rb><rt>sæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>山上</rb><rt>sæn-zông</rt><rb>落来</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>全族</rb><rt>djün-dzoh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ms. XXI.

<ruby><rb>住在</rb><rt>DJÜ-DZÆ</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>Nen-pin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚腊</rb><rt>Üô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>听见</rb><rt>tʽing-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚大林</rb><rt>Üô-da-ling</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一路</rb><rt>ih-lu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>许愿</rb><rt>hyü-nyün</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>毁灭</rb><rt>hwe-mih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>毁灭</rb><rt>hwe-mih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>盍马</rb><rt>ʽEh-mô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何珥</rb><rt>ʽO-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>红</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt><rb>边</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>介走</rb><rt>ka-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>绕转</rb><rt>nyiao-cün</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以东</u>地</rb><rt>Yi-tong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>锐气</rb><rt>ze-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>死在</rb><rt>si-dzæ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>厌憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>淡薄个</rb><rt>dæn-boh-go</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>火蛇</rb><rt>ho-dzô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>咬</rb><rt>ngao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-liao</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>驮过</rb><rt>do-ko</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>制造</rb><rt>cü-zao</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>火蛇</rb><rt>ho-dzô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挂来</rb><rt>kwô-læ</rt><rb>旗杆</rb><rt>gyi-ken</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt><rb>咬个</rb><rt>ngao-go</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>制造</rb><rt>cü-zao</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>铜蛇</rb><rt>dong-dzô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挂来</rb><rt>kwô-læ</rt><rb>旗杆</rb><rt>gyi-ken</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt><rb>咬个</rb><rt>ngao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>铜蛇</rb><rt>dong-dzô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>活了</rb><rt>weh-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿泊</rb><rt>O-boh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿泊</rb><rt>O-boh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以雅巴林</rb><rt>Yi-yüô-pô-ling</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>东边个</rb><rt>tong-pin-go</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒烈</rb><rt>Sah-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>边个</rb><rt>pin-go</rt><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚嫩</rb><rt>Üô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚嫩</rb><rt>Üô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>境界</rb><rt>kying-ka</rt><rb>流出来</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>流到</rb><rt>liu-tao</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚摩利</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li-go</rt><rb>交界</rb><rt>kao-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt><rb>书有</rb><rt>shü-yiu</rt><rb>记的</rb><rt>kyi-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>苏法</u>个</rb><rt>Su-fah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚协</rb><rt>Üô-yiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚嫩</rb><rt>Üô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>边个</rb><rt>pin-go</rt><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚珥</rb><rt>Üô-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>流落来</rb><rt>liu-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠近</rb><rt>kʽao-gying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>境界</rb><rt>kying-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>箇口</rb><rt>keh-kʽeo</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>唱歌</rb><rt>tsʽông-ko</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>井</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>涌出</rb><rt>üong-cʽih</rt><rb>好水</rb><rt>hao-shü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歌唱</rb><rt>ko-tsʽông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>井</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>圭</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>Tsiang-lao</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>极力</rb><rt>gyih-lih</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛大拿</rb><rt>Mô-da-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛大拿</rb><rt>Mô-da-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿哈列</rb><rt>Nô-hah-lih</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿哈列</rb><rt>Nô-hah-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴末</rb><rt>Pô-mah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴末</rb><rt>Pô-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟个</rb><rt>bing-yiang-go</rt><rb>岙里</rb><rt>ao-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比士迦</rb><rt>Pi-z-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-ting</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>对着</rb><rt>te-djôh</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打发</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>22</sup> 「<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>岔进</rb><rt>tsʽô-tsing</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>岔进</rb><rt>tsʽô-tsing</rt><rb>葡萄园里</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>井里个</rb><rt>tsing-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只用</rb><rt>tsih-yüong</rt><rb>大路</rb><rt>do-lu</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗许</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聚集</rb><rt>jü-jih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚哈斯</rb><rt>Üô-hah-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>打败</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚嫩</rb><rt>Üô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚泊</rb><rt>Üô-boh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚扪</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚扪</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>极其</rb><rt>gyih-gyi</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>属</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西宏</u>个</rb><rt>Si-ʽong-go</rt><rb>京城</rb><rt>kying-dzing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夺</rb><rt>deh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚扪</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>作过个</rb><rt>tsoh-ko-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>建造</rb><rt>kyin-zao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>发出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>火焰</rb><rt>ho-yin</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>烧掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚珥</rb><rt>Üô-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚嫩</rb><rt>Üô-neng</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>巴末</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-mah-go</rt><rb>诸侯</rb><rt>cü-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<u><ruby><rb>基抹</rb><rt>Kyi-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>败掉</rb><rt>ba-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>掳掠去</rb><rt>lo-liah-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底本</rb><rt>Ti-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>毁灭</rb><rt>hwe-mih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>毁掉</rb><rt>hwe-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪法</rb><rt>Nô-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米底巴</rb><rt>Mi-ti-pô</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窥探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅设</rb><rt>Yüô-sih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚摩利</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>噩</rb><rt>Ngoh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>率领</rb><rt>sah-ling</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以得来</rb><rt>Yi-teh-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交战</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>中</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>庉在</rb><rt>deng-dzæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希实本</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>中</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>甚至于</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-lôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ms. XXII.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>对着</rb><rt>te-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西拨</rb><rt>Si-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>极其</rb><rt>gyih-gyi</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>人数</rb><rt>nying-su</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米甸</u>个</rb><rt>Mi-din-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>吃完</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-wun</rt><rb>田里个</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>草</rb><rt>tsʽao</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西拨</u>个</rb><rt>Si-pah-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>河边个</rb><rt>ʽO-pin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比夺</rb><rt>Pi-deh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一班</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>遮盖</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt><rb>地面</rb><rt>di-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>对头</rb><rt>te-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>强如</rb><rt>gyiang-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>得胜</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt><rb>打败</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>祝福个</rb><rt>coh-foh-go</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>咒诅个</rb><rt>tsiu-cü-go</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米甸</u>个</rb><rt>Mi-din-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴兰</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-læn-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴勒</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-lah-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>今朝</rb><rt>kying-tsiao</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>宿在</rb><rt>soh-dzæ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>回报</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>；」<ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>ü-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米甸</u>个</rb><rt>Mi-din-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西拨</u>个</rb><rt>Si-pah-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>11</sup> 『<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一班</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遮盖</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt><rb>地面</rb><rt>di-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>祝福个</rb><rt>coh-foh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴勒</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-lah-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>本国</rb><rt>peng-koh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>弗许</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>起身</rb><rt>kyʽi-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴勒</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-lah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>尊贵个</rb><rt>tseng-kwe-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>西拨</rb><rt>Si-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>阻隔</rb><rt>tsu-kah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>顶大</rb><rt>ting-do</rt><rb>尊贵</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>都会</rb><rt>tu-we</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴勒</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-lah-go</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>满</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>屋个</rb><rt>oh-go</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>少</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>宿</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>一夜</rb><rt>ih-yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>夜里</rb><rt>yia-li</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若来</rb><rt>ziah-læ</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」
