<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>大怒</rb><rt>da-nu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>骑勒</rb><rt>gyi-leh</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>驴子</rb><rt>Li-ts</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>立来</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>宝剑</rb><rt>pao-tsin</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>岔进</rb><rt>tsʽô-tsing</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走转</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>葡萄园</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün</rt><rb>中央个</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>都有</rb><rt>tu-yiu</rt><rb>墙</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>驴子</rb><rt>Li-ts</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>贴着</rb><rt>tʽiah-djôh</rt><rb>墙</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴兰</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-læn-go</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>轧住</rb><rt>gah-djü</rt><rb>墙</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走过去点</rb><rt>tseo-ko-kyʽi-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>狭窄个</rb><rt>ʽah-tsah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>左边</rb><rt>tso-pin</rt><rb>右边</rb><rt>yiu-pin</rt><rb>呒路</rb><rt>m-lu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>转动</rb><rt>cün-dong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>驴子</rb><rt>Li-ts</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>困倒</rb><rt>kwʽeng-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>发怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>杖</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>驴子个</rb><rt>li-ts-go</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>轻慢</rb><rt>kyʽing-mæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>巴弗能够</rb><rt>pô-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>宝剑</rb><rt>pao-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>驴子</rb><rt>Li-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>岂弗是</rb><rt>kyʽi-feh-z</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>骑个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>今朝</rb><rt>kying-tsiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>过弗</rb><rt>ko-feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>M-yiu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴兰</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-læn-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>宝剑</rb><rt>pao-kyin</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>伛倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt><rb>扑在</rb><rt>pʽoh-dzæ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>走个</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>驴子</rb><rt>Li-ts</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>岔过</rb><rt>tsʽô-ko</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>岔过</rb><rt>tsʽô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>救活</rb><rt>kyiu-weh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>倒走转</rb><rt>tao-tseo-cün</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>Kʽo-yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>然而</rb><rt>jün-r</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>边界</rb><rt>pin-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚嫩</rb><rt>Üô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河边</rb><rt>ʽo-pin</rt><rb>属</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>切身</rb><rt>tsʽih-sing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>岂弗能够</rb><rt>kyʽi-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>尊贵</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>来东了</rb><rt>læ-tong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>口里</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列胡朔</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-wu-soh</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>献祭</rb><rt>hyin-tsi</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>送拨</rb><rt>song-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>送拨</rb><rt>song-peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来的个</rb><rt>læ-tih-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴力</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-lih-go</rt><rb>高地</rb><rt>kao-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>营寨个</rb><rt>ying-dza-go</rt><rb>外边</rb><rt>nga-pin</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ms. XXIII.

<u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>PÔ-LÆN</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>七座</rb><rt>tsʽih-zo</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>预备</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>七只</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七只</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴兰</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-læn-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>坛上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>烧化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>会着</rb><rt>we-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>光光</rb><rt>kwông-kwông</rt><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>会着</rb><rt>we-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>预备</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>七座</rb><rt>tsʽih-zo</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>每座</rb><rt>me-zo</rt><rb>坛上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt><rb>献过</rb><rt>hyin-ko</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>放在</rb><rt>fông-dzæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴兰</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-læn-go</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴勒</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-lah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴勒</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-lah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>烧化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歌词</rb><rt>ko-dz</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚兰</rb><rt>Üô-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>东边个</rb><rt>tong-pin-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>。』

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>咒诅个</rb><rt>tsiu-cü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>恶愫个</rb><rt>u-su-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>磐石上</rb><rt>bun-zah-zông</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>山顶上</rb><rt>sæn-ting-zông</rt><rb>望见</rb><rt>mông-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>独自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>列国</rb><rt>lih-koh</rt><rb>算进</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt><rb>在内</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能</rb><rt>neng</rt><rb>数过</rb><rt>shü-ko</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>四分之一</rb><rt>s-feng-ts-ih</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能</rb><rt>neng</rt><rb>算过</rb><rt>sön-ko</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>善人</rb><rt>jün-nying</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>结局</rb><rt>kyih-gyüoh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>反</rb><rt>fæn</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>小心</rb><rt>siao-sing</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>口里</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>别</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>单</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大众</rb><rt>da-cong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>琐腓</rb><rt>So-fi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比士迦</rb><rt>Pi-z-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>七座</rb><rt>tsʽih-zo</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>坛上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>会着</rb><rt>we-djôh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>会着</rb><rt>we-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>口里</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介讲</rb><rt>ka-kông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴勒</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-lah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲过</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歌词</rb><rt>ko-dz</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>起身</rb><rt>kyʽi-sing</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>西拨</rb><rt>Si-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>；

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>说谎</rb><rt>shih-hwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>悔改</rb><rt>hwe-kæ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话过</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt><rb>岂弗会</rb><rt>kyʽi-feh-we</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>讲过</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算数</rb><rt>sön-su</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>赐福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>反向</rb><rt>fæn-hyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>横行不法</rb><rt>wang-ying-peh-fah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>gyi-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>呼和</rb><rt>hwu-ʽo</rt><rb>王个</rb><rt>wông-go</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>刚强</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>野牛</rb><rt>yia-ngeo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>邪法</rb><rt>zia-fah</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>卜课</rb><rt>poh-kʽo</rt><rb>等事</rb><rt>teng-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>轮到</rb><rt>Leng-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做过</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雌</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>狮子</rb><rt>s-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跳起来</rb><rt>tʽiao-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>狮子</rb><rt>s-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弶着个</rb><rt>gyiang-djôh-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>打煞个</rb><rt>tang-sah-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>困倒</rb><rt>kwʽeng-tao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗用</rb><rt>feh-yüong</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗用</rb><rt>feh-yüong</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>话过</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>请来</rb><rt>Tsʽing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>旷野个</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>七座</rb><rt>tsʽih-zo</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>预备</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>七只</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七只</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴兰</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-læn-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>坛上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. XXIV.

<u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>PÔ-LÆN</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>法术</rb><rt>fah-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>庉间</rb><rt>deng-kæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歌词</rb><rt>ko-dz</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>比珥</u>个</rb><rt>Pi-r-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>Ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>闭的个</rb><rt>pi-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>全能</rb><rt>djün-neng</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>异像</rb><rt>yi-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>开的个</rb><rt>kʽæ-tih-go</rt></ruby>；」<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>5</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>庉处</rb><rt>deng-cʽü</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>华美</rb><rt>wô-me</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>布开</rb><rt>pu-kʽæ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>山岙</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>河边个</rb><rt>ʽo-pin-go</rt><rb>花园</rb><rt>hwô-yün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>种个</rb><rt>cong-go</rt><rb>沉香</rb><rt>dzing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>边沿个</rb><rt>pin-yin-go</rt><rb>柏香</rb><rt>pah-hyiang</rt><rb>木</rb><rt>moh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>溢出来</rb><rt>kah-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>发生起来</rb><rt>fah-seng-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>高如</rb><rt>kao-jü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚甲</rb><rt>Üô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>兴旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>刚强</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt><rb>犹之乎</rb><rt>yiu-ts-wu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>野牛</rb><rt>yia-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>吞吃</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>仇敌个</rb><rt>dziu-dih-go</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>敲断</rb><rt>kʽao-dön</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>骨头</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>弓</rb><rt>kong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箭</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>射煞</rb><rt>zih-sah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>伏的</rb><rt>boh-tih</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>狮子</rb><rt>s-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>困倒</rb><rt>kwʽeng-tao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雌</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>狮子</rb><rt>s-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>引动</rb><rt>ying-dong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>祝福</rb><rt>Coh-foh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt></ruby>。」』」

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>发怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>括括</rb><rt>kweh-kweh</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>本乡</rb><rt>peng-hyiang</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>至于</rb><rt>ts-ü</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>尊贵</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>尊贵</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差来个</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话过</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>满</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>过于</rb><rt>ko-ü</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歌词</rb><rt>ko-dz</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>Ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>闭的个</rb><rt>pi-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>知识</rb><rt>cü-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>全能</rb><rt>djün-neng</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>异像个</rb><rt>yi-ziang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>开的个</rb><rt>kʽæ-tih-go</rt></ruby>；」<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>近来</rb><rt>gying-læ</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>皇上</rb><rt>wông-zông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>执</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>之</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>圭</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兴起</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>打过</rb><rt>tang-ko</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>两边</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>吵乱个</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以东</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>丝珥</rb><rt>S-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得胜</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>权柄个</rb><rt>gyün-ping-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>剩落个</rb><rt>dzing-lôh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。」』」

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚玛力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歌词</rb><rt>ko-dz</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>亚玛力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>列国个</rb><rt>lih-koh-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>终于</rb><rt>cong-ü</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>灭亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基尼</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歌词</rb><rt>ko-dz</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>庉处</rb><rt>deng-cʽü</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>窠</rb><rt>kʽo</rt><rb>放在</rb><rt>fông-dzæ</rt><rb>磐石上</rb><rt>bun-zah-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基尼</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>衰败</rb><rt>sæ-ba</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚述</rb><rt>Üô-jih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>掳掠去</rb><rt>lo-liah-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歌词</rb><rt>ko-dz</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>？

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基底</rb><rt>Kyi-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海边</rb><rt>hæ-pin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>磨难</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚述</rb><rt>Üô-jih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>磨难</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯</rb><rt>Hyi-pah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>本乡</rb><rt>peng-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. XXV.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>失亭</rb><rt>Shih-ding</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女子</rb><rt>nyü-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>献祭</rb><rt>hyin-tsi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴力</rb><rt>Pô-lih</rt><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>发怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>对着</rb><rt>te-djôh</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吊煞</rb><rt>tiao-sah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>发个</rb><rt>fah-go</rt><rb>大怒</rb><rt>da-nu</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>止住</rb><rt>ts-djü</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>士师</rb><rt>z-s</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴力</rb><rt>Pô-lih</rt><rb>比珥</u>个</rb><rt>Pi-r-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟个</rb><rt>hyüong-di-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>孙子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以利亚撒</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非尼哈</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-ha</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>枪</rb><rt>tsʽiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>跟着</rb><rt>Keng-djôh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>戳煞</rb><rt>tsʽoh-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>止住了</rb><rt>ts-djü-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>遭</rb><rt>Tsao</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二万</rb><rt>nyi-væn</rt><rb>四千</rb><rt>s-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>11</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>孙子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非尼哈</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>息怒</rb><rt>sih-nu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>嫉妒之心</rb><rt>dzih-tu-ts-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>dzih-tu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>平安个</rb><rt>bing-en-go</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>Pih-ding</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>祭司个</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>嫉妒之心</rb><rt>dzih-tu-ts-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>杀个</rb><rt>sah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>心利</rb><rt>Sing-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒路</rb><rt>Sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>西缅</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>族长</rb><rt>dzoh-tsiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>杀个</rb><rt>sah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥斯比</rb><rt>Ko-s-pi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>苏珥</u>个</rb><rt>Su-r-go</rt><rb>女儿</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>17</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>奸计</rb><rt>kæn-kyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>诱惑</rb><rt>yiu-ʽoh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>牧伯个</rb><rt>moh-pah-go</rt><rb>女儿</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥斯比</rb><rt>Ko-s-pi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>遭</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Ms. XXVI.

<ruby><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>W̆ENG-YÜOH</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>Kæ</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>廿岁</rb><rt>nyiæn-shü</rt><rb>以上</rb><rt>yi-zông</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>交战个</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt><rb>靠近</rb><rt>kʽao-gying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河边</rb><rt>ʽo-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>廿岁</rb><rt>nyiæn-shü</rt><rb>以上</rb><rt>yi-zông</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>：

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>长子</rb><rt>tsiang-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u>；<u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈诺</rb><rt>Ha-noh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈诺</rb><rt>Ha-noh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴路</rb><rt>Pô-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴路</rb><rt>Pô-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>Joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希斯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-s-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希斯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-s-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦米</rb><rt>Kyüô-mi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦米</rb><rt>Kyüô-mi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>派个</rb><rt>pʽa-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四万</rb><rt>s-væn</rt><rb>三千</rb><rt>sæn-tsʽin</rt><rb>七百</rb><rt>tsʽih-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb><u>巴路</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-lu-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利押</rb><rt>Yi-li-iah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以利押</rb><rt>Yi-li-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼母利</rb><rt>Nyi-meo-li</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>大丹</rb><rt>Da-tæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚比兰</rb><rt>Üô-pi-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大丹</rb><rt>Da-tæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚比兰</rb><rt>Üô-pi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>名望</rb><rt>ming-vông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同党</rb><rt>dong-tông</rt><rb>叛逆</rb><rt>bun-nyih</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 此处原书错印为 Nyi-mo-li，后文皆为 Nyi-meo-li</sup>

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>开口</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽeo</rt><rb>吞吃</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一党</rb><rt>ih-tông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-liao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>烧灭</rb><rt>siao-mih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>哥拉</u>个</rb><rt>Ko-lah-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西缅</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>尼母利</u>个</rb><rt>Nyi-meo-li-go</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼母利</rb><rt>Nyi-meo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅悯</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-ming-go</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅悯</rb><rt>Yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅斤</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-kying-go</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅斤</rb><rt>Yüô-kying</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>Joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西剌</u>个</rb><rt>Si-lah-go</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西剌</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>沙罗</u>个</rb><rt>Sô-lo-go</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>沙罗</rb><rt>Sô-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西缅</u>个</rb><rt>Si-min-go</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二万</rb><rt>nyi-væn</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>洗分</rb><rt>Si-feng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>洗分</rb><rt>Si-feng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈基</rb><rt>Ha-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈基</rb><rt>Ha-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>书尼</rb><rt>Shü-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>书尼</rb><rt>Shü-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>Joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿斯尼</rb><rt>O-s-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿斯尼</rb><rt>O-s-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利</rb><rt>Yi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利</rb><rt>Yi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb><u>犹大</u>个</rb><rt>Yiu-da-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>珥</rb><rt>R</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿南</rb><rt>O-nen</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>珥</rb><rt>R</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿南</rb><rt>O-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法勒斯</rb><rt>Fah-lah-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法勒斯</rb><rt>Fah-lah-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹大</u>个</rb><rt>Yiu-da-go</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>派个</rb><rt>pʽa-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七万</rb><rt>tsʽih-væn</rt><rb>六千</rb><rt>loh-tsʽin</rt><rb>五百</rb><rt>ng-pah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>Joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅述</rb><rt>Yüô-jih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅述</rb><rt>Yüô-jih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伸仑</rb><rt>Shing-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伸仑</rb><rt>Shing-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以萨迦</rb><rt>Yi-sah-kyÜô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>派个</rb><rt>pʽa-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六万</rb><rt>loh-væn</rt><rb>四千</rb><rt>s-tsʽin</rt><rb>三百</rb><rt>sæn-pah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西布伦</rb><rt>Si-pu-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西烈</u>个</rb><rt>Si-lih-go</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西烈</rb><rt>Si-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以伦</rb><rt>Yi-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以伦</rb><rt>Yi-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅列</rb><rt>Yüô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅列</rb><rt>Yüô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希弗</rb><rt>Hyi-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西罗非哈</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>女儿</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>女儿</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>挪亚</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>曷拉</rb><rt>ʽEh-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>密迦</rb><rt>Mih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>得撒</rb><rt>Teh-sah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>53</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>分拨</rb><rt>feng-peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名数</rb><rt>ming-su</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>撮个</rb><rt>tsʽah-go</rt><rb>指末头</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>57</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>派个</rb><rt>pʽa-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革顺</rb><rt>Kah-jing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革顺</rb><rt>Kah-jing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥辖</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥辖</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米拉利</rb><rt>Mi-lah-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米拉利</rb><rt>Mi-lah-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>。
