<sup>60</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿答</rb><rt>Nô-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚比户</rb><rt>üô-pi-wu</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以大玛</rb><rt>Yi-da-mô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>61</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拿答</rb><rt>Nô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚比户</rb><rt>Üô-pi-wu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>诚心个</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。

<sup>62</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>派个</rb><rt>pʽa-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>一月</rb><rt>ih-yüih</rt><rb>以上个</rb><rt>yi-zông-go</rt><rb>男子</rb><rt>nen-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二万</rb><rt>nyi-væn</rt><rb>三千</rb><rt>sæn-tsʽin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>派进</rb><rt>pʽa-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>63</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>派个</rb><rt>pʽa-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt><rb>靠近</rb><rt>kʽao-gying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶利哥</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河边</rb><rt>ʽo-pin</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>64</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>派个</rb><rt>pʽa-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>在内</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>65</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>话过</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>Djü-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孚尼</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-leh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-lôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ms. XXVII.

<u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>IAH-SEH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族个</rb><rt>dzoh-go</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>玛吉</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-kyih-go</rt><rb>曾孙</rb><rt>tseng-seng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb><u>基列</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-go</rt><rb>孙子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>基弗</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-fah-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西罗非哈</u>个</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha-go</rt><rb>女儿</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>挪亚</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>曷拉</rb><rt>ʽEh-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>密迦</rb><rt>Mih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>得撒</rb><rt>Teh-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>会幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>3</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>父亲</rb><rt>vu-tsʽing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一党</rb><rt>ih-tông</rt><rb>在内</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-lôh</rt></ruby>；

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>父亲</rb><rt>vu-tsʽing</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>叔伯</rb><rt>shoh-pah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>请问</rb><rt>tsʽing-meng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>7</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>西罗非哈</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女儿</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>叔伯</rb><rt>shoh-pah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>父亲个</rb><rt>vu-tsʽing-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>女儿</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>女儿</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>叔伯</rb><rt>shoh-pah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>叔伯</rb><rt>shoh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>本族个</rb><rt>peng-dzoh-go</rt><rb>亲房</rb><rt>tsʽing-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>定例</rb><rt>ding-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚巴琳</rb><rt>Üô-pô-ling</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>看过仔</rb><rt>Kʽen-ko-ts</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>归到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>汛</u>个</rb><rt>Sing-go</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>争斗</rb><rt>tsang-teo</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>尊重</rb><rt>tseng-djong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>汛</u>个</rb><rt>Sing-go</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦铁</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>米利巴</u>个</rb><rt>Mi-li-pô-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。）」

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>性命个</rb><rt>sing-ming-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>灵个</rb><rt>ling-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>Kʽo-yi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>进出</rb><rt>tsing-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>免得</rb><rt>min-teh</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>羊牧个</rb><rt>yiang-moh-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>感动个</rb><rt>ken-dong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>按</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会个</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>尊贵</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依顺</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>请问</rb><rt>tsʽing-meng</rt><rb>乌陵</rb><rt>U-ling</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定个</rb><rt>ding-go</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>进来</rb><rt>tsing-læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>按手</rb><rt>en-siu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. XXVIII.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>Tông-kæ</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>喷香个</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>火祭</rb><rt>ho-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>敬心</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定好个</rb><rt>ding-hao-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>火祭个</rb><rt>ho-tsi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>呒毛病</rb><rt>m-mao-bing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>满</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>两只</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>平常个</rb><rt>bing-djông-go</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>Ih-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>早上</rb><rt>tsao-zông</rt><rb>献个</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>晚上</rb><rt>mæn-zông</rt><rb>献个</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>细面粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>四股之一</rb><rt>s-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>榨出个</rb><rt>tsô-cʽih-go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>；

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>平常个</rb><rt>bing-djông-go</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定个</rb><rt>ding-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>喷香</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang</rt><rb>献拨</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>火祭</rb><rt>ho-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>献</rb><rt>Hyin</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>奠酒</rb><rt>din-tsiu</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>四股</rb><rt>s-kwu</rt><rb>之一</rb><rt>ts-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>圣所</rb><rt>Sing-sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>奠</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>烧酒</rb><rt>siao-tsiu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>第二只</rb><rt>Di-nyi-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>晚上</rb><rt>mæn-zông</rt><rb>献个</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>献个</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奠酒</rb><rt>din-tsiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>早上</rb><rt>tsao-zông</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>喷香个</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>火祭</rb><rt>ho-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>En-sih-nyih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>两只</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>呒毛病</rb><rt>m-mao-bing</rt><rb>弗满</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年个</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的</rb><rt>ʽo-tih</rt><rb>细面粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股之二</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-r</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奠</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>安息日个</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih-go</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>平常</rb><rt>bing-djông</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>奠酒</rb><rt>din-tsiu</rt><rb>之外个</rb><rt>ts-nga-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>月头</rb><rt>Yüih-deo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>两只</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七只</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>呒毛病</rb><rt>m-mao-bing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>满</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>一年个</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>每只</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的个</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>细面粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股之三</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-sæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每只</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的个</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>细面粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股之二</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-r</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>每只</rb><rt>Me-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的个</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>细面粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>喷香个</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>Sô</rt><rb>奠个</rb><rt>din-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt><rb>半欣</rb><rt>pun-hying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>四股之一</rb><rt>s-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>到头</rb><rt>tao-deo</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>月个</rb><rt>yüih-go</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>赎罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>平常</rb><rt>bing-djông</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>配拢</rb><rt>pʽe-long</rt><rb>奠个</rb><rt>din-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>Dzæ</rt><rb>正月</rb><rt>tsing-yüih</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>呒酵水</rb><rt>m-kao-shü</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>；

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>第一日</rb><rt>Di-ih-nyih</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>圣会</rb><rt>sing-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>苦工</rb><rt>kwʽu-kong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>Tông-kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>两只</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七只</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>满</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>一年个</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>呒毛病</rb><rt>m-mao-bing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>七只</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>每只</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>；

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>赎罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>：

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>献个</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>早上个</rb><rt>tsao-zông-go</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>平常个</rb><rt>bing-djông-go</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>七日</rb><rt>Tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>献个</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>喷香个</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>平常个</rb><rt>bing-djông-go</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>奠个</rb><rt>din-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>献个</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>第七日</rb><rt>Di-tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>圣会</rb><rt>sing-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>苦工</rb><rt>kwʽu-kong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>献</rb><rt>Hyin</rt><rb>尝新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>七七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>新个</rb><rt>sing-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>圣会</rb><rt>sing-we</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>苦工</rb><rt>kwʽu-kong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>喷香个</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>两只</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七只</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>满</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>一年个</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>配拢个</rb><rt>pʽe-long-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的个</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>细面粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>每只</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>十股之三</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-sæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>每只</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>十股之二</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-r</rt></ruby>；

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>Ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>赎罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>除</rb><rt>Djü</rt><rb>平常个</rb><rt>bing-djông-go</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>奠个</rb><rt>din-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>献个</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>呒</rb><rt>m</rt><rb>毛病</rb><rt>mao-bing</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. XXIX.

<ruby><rb>七月</rb><rt>TSʽIH-YÜIH</rt><rb>初一</rb><rt>tsʽu-ih</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>圣会</rb><rt>sing-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>苦工</rb><rt>kwʽu-kong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>号筒个</rb><rt>ʽao-dong-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>香气</rb><rt>hyiang-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一头</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七只</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>弗满</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>呒毛病个</rb><rt>m-mao-bing-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>配拢个</rb><rt>pʽe-long-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的个</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>细面粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>十股之三</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-sæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>十股之二</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-r</rt></ruby>；

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>七只</rb><rt>Tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>每只</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>；

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>赎罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>定例</rb><rt>ding-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>月头</rb><rt>yüih-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>献个</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>平常</rb><rt>bing-djông</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奠酒</rb><rt>din-tsiu</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>香气</rb><rt>hyiang-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>七月</rb><rt>Tsʽih-yüih</rt><rb>初十</rb><rt>tsʽu-jih</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>圣会</rb><rt>sing-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>苦苦</rb><rt>kwʽu-kwʽu</rt><rb>斋戒</rb><rt>tsæ-kyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>香气</rb><rt>hyiang-kyʽi</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七只</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>弗满</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>呒毛病个</rb><rt>m-mao-bing-go</rt></ruby>；

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>配拢</rb><rt>pʽe-long</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的个</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>细面粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>十股之三</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>十股之二</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-r</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>七只</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>每只</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>；

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>Ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>犯罪祭</rb><rt>væn-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>赎罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>平常</rb><rt>bing-djông</rt><rb>烧化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奠</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>之外个</rb><rt>ts-nga-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>七月</rb><rt>Tsʽih-yüih</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>圣会</rb><rt>sing-we</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>苦工</rb><rt>kwʽu-kong</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. XXXI.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米甸</u>个</rb><rt>Mi-din-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>报仇</rb><rt>pao-dziu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>挑选</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>驮军器个</rb><rt>do-kyüing-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>交战个</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>报</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>仇</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>全军</rb><rt>djün-kyüing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>挑选</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>共数</rb><rt>gong-su</rt><rb>一万</rb><rt>ih-væn</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>交战个</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>一千个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以利亚撒</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非尼哈</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-ha</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>圣所个</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>太长声个</rb><rt>tʽa-dziang-sing-go</rt><rb>号筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>带勒去</rb><rt>ta-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 此处原文错印为 Fi-ni-ha，前后文均为 Fi-nyi-ha</sup>

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>男子</rb><rt>nen-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>在内个</rb><rt>dzæ-nen-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以未</rb><rt>Yi-vi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利金</rb><rt>Li-kying</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>苏珥</rb><rt>Su-r</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>户珥</rb><rt>Wu-r</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利巴</rb><rt>Li-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>比珥</u>个</rb><rt>Pi-r-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>宝剑</rb><rt>pao-kyin</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米甸</u>个</rb><rt>Mi-din-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>烧掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉个</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>营盘</rb><rt>ying-bun</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>带来</rb><rt>ta-læ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>掳掠个</rb><rt>lo-liah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟个</rb><rt>bing-yiang-go</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河边个</rb><rt>ʽo-pin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>公会个</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>营寨外</rb><rt>ying-dza-nga</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>带领</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>千夫长</rb><rt>tsʽin-fu-tsiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百夫长</rb><rt>pah-fu-tsiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>发怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>，

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>救活</rb><rt>kyiu-weh</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴兰</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-læn-go</rt><rb>计较</rb><rt>kyi-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>比珥</u>个</rb><rt>Pi-r-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>故此</rb><rt>Kwu-tsʽ</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>男子</rb><rt>nen-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>同床</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt><rb>过个</rb><rt>ko-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>同床过</rb><rt>dong-zông-ko</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救活</rb><rt>kyiu-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>摸着</rb><rt>moh-djoh</rt><rb>尸首个</rb><rt>s-siu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>掳掠个</rb><rt>lo-liah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>每件</rb><rt>me-gyin</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>皮货</rb><rt>bi-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>羊毛</rb><rt>yiang-mao</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>树木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>交战个</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>律法个</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>规条</rb><rt>kwe-diao</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>金</rb><rt>Kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>银</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>铜</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>铁</rb><rt>tʽih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>锡</rb><rt>sih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>铅</rb><rt>kʽæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>锻炼</rb><rt>tön-lin</rt></ruby>；

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>锻炼</rb><rt>tön-lin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>净水</rb><rt>zing-shü</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>锻炼</rb><rt>tön-lin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>26</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>公会个</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口个</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>掳来个</rb><rt>lo-læ-go</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>两股</rb><rt>liang-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>交战个</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>又要</rb><rt>Yi-iao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>兵将</rb><rt>ping-tsiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>进贡个</rb><rt>tsing-kong-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>五百</rb><rt>ng-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>收来个</rb><rt>siu-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>摇祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>又要</rb><rt>Yi-iao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>五十个</rb><rt>ng-jih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>驮个</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>收来个</rb><rt>siu-læ-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>圣所个</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>除</rb><rt>Djü</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>leh</rt><rb>兵将</rb><rt>ping-tsiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>夺来个</rb><rt>deh-læ-go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六十七万</rb><rt>loh-jih-tsʽih-væn</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>；

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>七万</rb><rt>Tsʽih-væn</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>；

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>六万</rb><rt>Loh-væn</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>匹</rb><rt>pʽih</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一万</rb><rt>ih-væn</rt><rb>六千</rb><rt>loh-tsʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>进贡</rb><rt>tsing-kong</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>二</rb><rt>nyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>47</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>无论</rb><rt>vu-leng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>圣所个</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>分个</rb><rt>feng-go</rt></ruby>。
