### Ms. XXXII.

<ruby><rb><u>流便</u>个</rb><rt>LIU-BIN-GO</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>迦得</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅设</u>地</rb><rt>Yüô-sih-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>牲口个</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>流便</u>个</rb><rt>Liu-bin-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>公会个</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>3</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>亚大录</rb><rt>Üô-da-loh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>底本</rb><rt>Ti-peng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅设</rb><rt>Yüô-sih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>宁拉</rb><rt>Nying-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以利亚利</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>示班</rb><rt>Z-pæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>尼破</rb><rt>Nyi-pʽo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>比稳</rb><rt>Pi-w̆eng</rt></ruby></u>，

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>攻打个</rb><rt>kong-tang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>流便</u>个</rb><rt>Liu-bin-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>交战个</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>还好</rb><rt>wa-hao</rt><rb>坐在</rb><rt>zo-dzæ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>倒锐气</rb><rt>tao-ze-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地吗</rb><rt>di-ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>从前</rb><rt>Dzong-zin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦铁</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt><rb>巴尼亚</rb><rt>Pô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窥探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以实各</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-jih-koh-go</rt><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>倒锐气</rb><rt>tao-ze-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>发怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>11</sup> 『<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出来个</rb><rt>cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>廿岁</rb><rt>nyiæn-shü</rt><rb>以上个</rb><rt>yi-zông-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>断</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>跟从</rb><rt>keng-dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基尼洗</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶孚尼</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>跟从</rb><rt>keng-dzong</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一代</rb><rt>ih-dæ</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>犯罪个</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>继续</rb><rt>kyi-djôh</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>兴起</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi</rt><rb>一班</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>恶人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>发怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>跟从</rb><rt>keng-dzong</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又会</rb><rt>yi-we</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>羊圈</rb><rt>yiang-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>建造</rb><rt>kyin-zao</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>军器</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>庉来</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>坚固个</rb><rt>kyin-kwu-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>免得</rb><rt>min-teh</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>土人</rb><rt>tʽu-nying</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>朝东个</rb><rt>dziao-tong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>军器</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>兵将个</rb><rt>ping-tsiang-go</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>介做</rb><rt>ka-tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>况且</rb><rt>hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>羊圈</rb><rt>yiang-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>建造</rb><rt>kyin-zao</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲过个</rb><rt>kông-ko-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>流便</u>个</rb><rt>Liu-bin-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>羊队</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>庉在</rb><rt>deng-dzæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基列</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>军器</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>交战个</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>关着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>迦得</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>流便</u>个</rb><rt>Liu-bin-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>军器</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前个</rb><rt>min-zin-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>军器</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>军器</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西宏</u>个</rb><rt>Si-ʽong-go</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>噩</u>个</rb><rt>Ngoh-go</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>地界个</rb><rt>di-ka-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>建造</rb><rt>kyin-zao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底本</rb><rt>Ti-peng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚大录</rb><rt>Üô-da-loh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚罗珥</rb><rt>Üô-lo-r</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>押律</rb><rt>Iah-lih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>说反</rb><rt>Shih-fæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅设</rb><rt>Yüô-sih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>约比哈</rb><rt>Iah-pi-ha</rt></ruby></u>；

<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>伯宁拉</rb><rt>Pah-nying-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伯哈兰</rb><rt>Pah-ha-læn</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>造圈</rb><rt>zao-gyin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>建造</rb><rt>kyin-zao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以利亚利</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基列亭</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-ding</rt></ruby></u>；

<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>尼破</rb><rt>Nyi-pʽo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>巴力免</rb><rt>Pô-lih-min</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>悉玛</rb><rt>Sih-mô</rt></ruby></u>（<ruby><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>换过</rb><rt>wun-ko</rt></ruby>）；<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>建造个</rb><rt>kyin-zao-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb><u>玛拿西</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>玛吉</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-kyih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉的个</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>玛拿西</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛吉</rb><rt>Mô-kyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>庉来</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb><u>玛拿西</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>睚珥</rb><rt>Yiæ-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>称</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>睚珥</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-r-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>42</sup> <u><ruby><rb>挪巴</rb><rt>Nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基纳</rb><rt>Kyi-nah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>属</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基纳</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-nah-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>称</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪巴</rb><rt>Nô-pô</rt></ruby></u>。

---

### Ms. XXXIII.

<ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH-GO</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>队兵</rb><rt>de-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>记录</rb><rt>kyi-loh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>正月</rb><rt>Tsing-yüih</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>兰塞</rb><rt>Læn-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt><rb>后</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>第二日</rb><rt>di-nyi-nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>舞脚</rb><rt>vu-kyiah</rt><rb>撩手</rb><rt>liao-siu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>杀掉过个</rb><rt>sah-diao-ko-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>头生个</rb><rt>deo-sang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>征伐</rb><rt>tsing-vah</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>兰塞</rb><rt>Læn-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>搭营寨</rb><rt>tah-ying-dza</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>疏割</rb><rt>Su-keh</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>疏割</rb><rt>Su-keh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>边沿个</rb><rt>pin-yin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以藤</rb><rt>Yi-deng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以藤</rb><rt>Yi-deng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>走转</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴力洗分</rb><rt>Pô-lih-si-feng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相对个</rb><rt>siang-te-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比哈希录</rb><rt>Pi-ha-hyi-loh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>密夺</rb><rt>Mih-dah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比哈希录</rb><rt>Pi-ha-hyi-loh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以藤</rb><rt>Yi-deng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以琳</rb><rt>Yi-ling</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以琳</rb><rt>Yi-ling</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>株</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>枣树</rb><rt>tsao-jü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚巴琳</rb><rt>Üô-pô-ling</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>边个</rb><rt>pin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>边</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯耶西末</rb><rt>Pah-yiæ-si-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯士亭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-z-ding</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>51</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>Pih-iao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>雕刻</rb><rt>tiao-kʽeh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>铸出个</rb><rt>cü-cʽih-go</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拆毁</rb><rt>tsʽah-hwe</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>高地上个</rb><rt>kao-di-zông-go</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉在</rb><rt>deng-dzæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt><rb>指末头</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>多个</rb><rt>to-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>缺个</rb><rt>kyʽüih-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撮出</rb><rt>tsʽah-cʽih</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>指末头</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>箇地个</rb><rt>keh-di-go</rt><rb>土人</rb><rt>tʽu-nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>剩落个</rb><rt>dzing-lôh-go</rt><rb>土人</rb><rt>tʽu-nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>磨难</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>腰胁里</rb><rt>iao-hyih-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>注定</rb><rt>cü-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>降灾</rb><rt>kyüông-tsæ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>土人</rb><rt>tʽu-nying</rt><rb>反向</rb><rt>fæn-hyiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。』」

---

### Ms. XXXIV.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业个</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>南边个</rb><rt>Nen-pin-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是从</rb><rt>z-dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>汛</u>个</rb><rt>Sing-go</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以东</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>盐海个</rb><rt>Yin-hæ-go</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>朝东</rb><rt>dziao-tong</rt><rb>为止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>绕转</rb><rt>nyiao-cün</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚克拉滨</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-kʽah-lah-ping-go</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>汛</rb><rt>Sing</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦铁巴尼亚</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih-Pô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈萨</rb><rt>Ha-sah</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>亚达</rb><rt>Üô-dah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>押们</rb><rt>Iah-meng</rt></ruby></u>；

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>押们</rb><rt>Iah-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>转弯</rb><rt>cün-w̆æn</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>为止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>西边</rb><rt>Si-pin</rt><rb>以</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>大 海</rb><rt>da-hæ</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>界</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>西边</rb><rt>Si-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>Poh-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>da</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>转</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何珥</rb><rt>ʽO-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>；

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何珥</rb><rt>ʽO-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>转</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈末</rb><rt>Ha-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西达</rb><rt>Si-deh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>；

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>Di-ka</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>锡分</rb><rt>Sih-feng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈萨以难</rb><rt>Ha-sah-yi-næn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈萨以难</rb><rt>Ha-sah-yi-næn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示番</rb><rt>Z-fæn</rt></ruby></u>；

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示番</rb><rt>Z-fæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>延到</rb><rt>yin-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>立拉</rb><rt>Lih-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>绕转</rb><rt>nyiao-cün</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚延</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-yin-go</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又要</rb><rt>yi-iao</rt><rb>延到</rb><rt>yin-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基尼烈</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>之</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>Di-ka</rt><rb>又要</rb><rt>yi-iao</rt><rb>落去</rb><rt>lôh-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>盐海</rb><rt>Yin-hæ</rt><rb>为止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽeh</rt><rb>指末头</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业个</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>九个</rb><rt>kyiu-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby>：

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>出起</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」

### Ms. XXXV.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河边</rb><rt>ʽo-pin</rt><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>四面个</rb><rt>s-min-go</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>Dzing-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>货物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</u>个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>派定</rb><rt>pʽa-ding</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>无意</rb><rt>vu-i</rt><rb>偶凑</rb><rt>neo-tsʽeo</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>逃命</rb><rt>Dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>报仇个</rb><rt>pao-dziu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>派定个</rb><rt>pʽa-ding-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六座</rb><rt>loh-zo</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>Dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>派定</rb><rt>pʽa-ding</rt><rb>三座</rb><rt>sæn-zo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt><rb>三座</rb><rt>sæn-zo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>逃命</rb><rt>Dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>外来</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt><rb>其中个</rb><rt>gyi-cong-go</rt><rb>出门</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>无意</rb><rt>vu-i</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>铁个</rb><rt>tʽih-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>打人</rb><rt>tang-nying</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>正</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>木器</rb><rt>moh-kyʽi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>报仇个</rb><rt>Pao-dziu-go</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>怨气</rb><rt>ün-kyʽi</rt><rb>推其</rb><rt>tʽe-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>埋伏间</rb><rt>ma-voh-kæn</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>；

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>怨</rb><rt>ün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打个</rb><rt>tang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>报仇个</rb><rt>pao-dziu-go</rt><rb>一碰着</rb><rt>ih-pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>除其</rb><rt>djü-gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>怨仇</rb><rt>ün-dziu</rt><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>推其</rb><rt>tʽe-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>埋伏个</rb><rt>ma-voh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>ang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>偶凑</rb><rt>neo-tsʽeo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>ang</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>怨仇</rb><rt>ün-dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>有意</rb><rt>yiu-i</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>：

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>打个</rb><rt>tang-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>报仇个</rb><rt>pao-dziu-go</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>公会</rb><rt>Kong-we</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>报仇个</rb><rt>pao-dziu-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>躲避过个</rb><rt>to-bi-ko-go</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>诚心个</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>傅过个</rb><rt>fu-ko-go</rt><rb>祭司长</rb><rt>tsi-s-tsiang</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>；

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>况且</rb><rt>Hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>报仇个</rb><rt>pao-dziu-go</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>随手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>庉来</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>祭司长</rb><rt>tsi-s-tsiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>祭司长</rb><rt>tsi-s-tsiang</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>以上</rb><rt>Yi-zông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>载的个</rb><rt>tsæ-tih-go</rt><rb>规条</rb><rt>kwe-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>审判个</rb><rt>sing-pʽun-go</rt><rb>定例</rb><rt>ding-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉个</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两三个</rb><rt>liang-sæn-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证个</rb><rt>te-tsing-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>死罪个</rb><rt>si-ze-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>躲</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城个</rb><rt>dzing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司长</rb><rt>tsi-s-tsiang</rt><rb>未死</rb><rt>vi-si</rt><rb>之前</rb><rt>ts-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>本乡</rb><rt>peng-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉的个</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>血个</rb><rt>hyüih-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只好</rb><rt>tsih-hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>犯罪个</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>断</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉的个</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。」

### Ms. XXXVI.

<u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>IAH-SEH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>孙子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>玛吉</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-kyih-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>族长</rb><rt>dzoh-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>族长</rb><rt>dzoh-tsiang</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt><rb>指末头</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt><rb>分拨</rb><rt>feng-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西罗非哈</u>个</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>女儿</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>减落</rb><rt>kæn-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>减落</rb><rt>kæn-loh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>禧年</rb><rt>hyi-nyin</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>减落个</rb><rt>kæn-lôh-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>属个</rb><rt>joh-go</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>减落</rb><rt>kæn-lôh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西罗非哈</u>个</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha-go</rt><rb>女儿</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>Kʽo-yi</rt><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>只要</rb><rt>tsih-iao</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>移</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>无论</rb><rt>vu-leng</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>女儿</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>移过</rb><rt>yi-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb><u>西罗非哈</u>个</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha-go</rt><rb>女儿</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb><u>西罗非哈</u>个</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha-go</rt><rb>女儿</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>得撒</rb><rt>Tah-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>曷拉</rb><rt>ʽEh-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>密迦</rb><rt>Mih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亚</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>叔伯个</rb><rt>soh-pah-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>玛拿西</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>以上</rb><rt>Yi-zông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>载的个</rb><rt>tsæ-tih-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt></ruby>。
